AbortSignal): Promise<PublicResponse>;\n}\n\nfunction ipv4Number(address: string): number {\n  return address.split('.').reduce((value, part) => value * 256 + Number(part), 0);\n}\nfunction ipv4In(address: number, base: string, bits: number): boolean {\n  const size = 2 ** (32 - bits);\n  return Math.floor(address / size) === Math.floor(ipv4Number(base) / size);\n}\nfunction ipv6Number(address: string): bigint {\n  const [left = '', right] = address.split('::');\n  const first = left ? left.split(':') : [];\n  const last = right ? right.split(':') : [];\n  const groups = right === undefined ? first : [...first, ...Array<string>(8 - first.length - last.length).fill('0'), ...last];\n  return groups.reduce((value, part) => (value << 16n) | BigInt(`0x${part}`), 0n);\n}\nfunction ipv6In(address: bigint, base: string, bits: number): boolean {\n  const shift = BigInt(128 - bits);\n  return address >> shift === ipv6Number(base) >> shift;\n}\n\n/** 连接前拒绝本地、文档、多播及地址转换网段。 */\nexport function isPublicAddress(address: string): boolean {\n  const family = isIP(address);\n  if (family === 4) {\n    const value = ipv4Number(address);\n    return !(\n      [\n        ['0.0.0.0', 8],\n        ['10.0.0.0', 8],\n        ['100.64.0.0', 10],\n        ['127.0.0.0', 8],\n        ['169.254.0.0', 16],\n        ['172.16.0.0', 12],\n        ['192.0.0.0', 24],\n ['2001:db8::', 32],\n        ['2002::', 16],\n        ['3fff::', 20],\n      ] as const\n    ).some(([base, bits]) => ipv6In(value, base, bits))\n  );\n}\n\nexport function parsePublicUrl(input: string): URL {\n  if (input.length > 2048 || [...input].some((character) => character.charCodeAt(0) <= 32 || character.charCodeAt(0) === 127))\n    throw new Error('公开网址格式无效');\n  const url = new URL(input);\n  if (url.protocol !== 'https:' || url.port !== '' || url.username || url.password)\n    throw new Error('公开文档仅允许无凭据的 HTTPS 443 地址');\n  const hostname = /\\.(localhost|local|internal|home|lan|test|invalid|example|onion)$/u.test(hostname)\n  )\n    throw new Error('不允许本地或保留域名');\n  if (isIP(hostname) && !isPublicAddress(hostname)) throw new Error('不允许私网或保留地址');\n  if (!isIP(hostname) && !hostname.includes('.')) throw new Error('公开文档需要完整公网域名');\n  url.hash = '';\n  return url;\n}\n\nfunction abortable<T>(promise: Promise<T>, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const abort = () => reject(new Error('公开文档加载超时或已取消'));\n    if (signal.aborted) return abort();\n    signal.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true });\n    promise.then(resolve, reject).finally(() => signal.removeEventListener('abort', abort));\n  });\n}\n\nconst dependencies: PublicFetchDependencies = {\n  resolve: (hostname) => lookup(hostname, { all: true, verbatim: true }),\n  request: (url, address, signal) =>\n    new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n      const chunks: Buffer[] = [];\n      let received = 0;\n      const request = httpsRequest(\n        url,\n        {\n          method: 'GET',\n          family: address.family,\n          rejectUnauthorized: true,\n          agent: false,\n          signal,\n          maxHeaderSize: 16384,\n          headers: { Accept: 'text/html', 'Accept-Encoding': 'identity', 'User-Agent': 'WebStudioLab-ReadonlyDocument/1.0' },\n          // Host 与 SNI 保持原域名；lookup 不能再次解析到 DNS 重绑定后的地址。\n          lookup: (_hostname, _options, callback) => callback(null, address.address, address.family),\n        },\n        (response) => {\n          const declaredLength = Number(response.headers['content-length']);\n          if (Number.isFinite(declaredLength) && declaredLength > MAX_SOURCE_BYTES) {\n            response.destroy(new Error('公开文档响应超过 2 MiB'));\n          }\n          response.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {\n            received += chunk.length;\n            if (received > MAX_SOURCE_BYTES) response.destroy(new Error('公开文档响应超过 2 MiB'));\n            else chunks.push(chunk);\n          });\n          response.on('error', reject);\n          response.on('end', () =>\n            resolve({\n              status: response.statusCode ?? 0,\n              headers: Object.fromEntries(\n                Object.entries(response.headers).map(([key, value]) => [key, Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(', ') : value]),\n              ),\n              body: Buffer.concat(chunks),\n            }),\n          );\n        },\n      );\n      request.on('error', reject);\n      request.end();\n    }),\n};\n\nexport async function fetchPublicDocument(\n  input: string,\n  fetchDependencies: PublicFetchDependencies = dependencies,\n  signal = AbortSignal.timeout(REQUEST_DEADLINE_MS),\n): Promise<PublicDocument> {\n  const requestedUrl = parsePublicUrl(input).href;\n  let url = new URL(requestedUrl);\n  for (let redirects = 0; redirects <= { useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';\n\n/** 极小的外部状态容器：状态更新只通过纯函数完成，便于单独测试。 */\nexport interface Store<S> {\n  get(): S;\n  set(update: (state: S) => S): void;\n  subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;\n}\n\nexport function createStore<S>(initial: S): Store<S> {\n  let state = initial;\n  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();\n  return {\n    get: () => state,\n    set(update) {\n      const next = update(state);\n      if (next === state) return;\n      state = next;\n      for (const listener of listeners) listener();\n    },\n    subscribe(listener) {\n      listeners.add(listener);\n      return () => {\n        listeners.delete(listener);\n      };\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/** selector 必须返回状态中已有的引用或原始值，否则会触发无限重渲染。 */\nexport function useStore<S, T>(store: Store<S>, selector: (state: S) => T): T {\n  return useSyncExternalStore(store.subscribe, () => selector(store.get()));\n}\n","usedDeprecatedRules":[]},{"filePath":"/Users/huaodong/workspace/_worktrees/pr-ready-20261013/docs/acceptance/integration-20261008/sources/11-task-5/committed-fallback/apps/desktop/src/renderer/src/lib/time.ts","messages":[{"ruleId":null,"fatal":true,"severity":2,"message":"Parsing };\n}\n\n/** 要求修改：记录原因；后续修改通过新的任务版本进行，不改写这次 run。 */\nexport function requestChanges(record: RunRecord, note: string, now: Date): RunRecord {\n  if (!canReview(record)) throw new Error('只有通过固定验收、等待审阅的 */\nexport async function captureGuestPage(\n  binary: string,\n  captureId = randomUUID(),\n  options: { startStoppedSandbox: boolean } = { startStoppedSandbox: false },\n): let bytes = 0;\n    let failure: GuestBrowserError | null = null;\n    const chunks: Buffer[] = [];\n    let killTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;\n    const cancel = (code: string, message: string) => {\n      if (failure) return;\n      failure = new GuestBrowserError(code, message, false);\n      // EOF 请求 guest finally 关闭浏览器；只结束本次本地传输，不以此声称远端已清理。\n      child.stdin.end();\n = false): Promise<{ code: number | null; frames: unknown[]; stderr: string }> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const file = fileURLToPath(new URL('./guest-browser.cjs', import.meta.url));\n    const child = spawn(process.execPath, nodeEval ? ['-e', fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')] : [file], {\n      stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'],\n    });\n    let stdout = '';\n    let stderr = '';\n    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {\n      child.kill('SIGKILL');\n      reject(new Error('协议子进程未退出'));\n    }, 5000);\n    child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => {\n      stdout += String(chunk);\n    });\n    child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) =>
